But `np.convolve` uses linear convolution with kernel not centered? For symmetric kernel k index 0..2r centered at r; full index m = sum hp[j]k[m-j]. At original hp center index r+i, desired m=r+i. same output index i? full[(r) + i?]. Same output index p = full[p+r]. For p=i, full[i+r]. Good. So take first n. Perfect.

Now, maybe `np.pad(mode='edge')` for r=0? if r=0 returns same. okay.

One issue: `project_sum_box` quick branch subtracts mean even if z out of bounds but h within. Suppose z all 2, n=100, S=50. h = 2 - (200-50)/100=1.5, max >1 => not quick, bisection. Bisection: lo=min-1=1, hi=max+1=3. At tau, h=clip(2-tau). Need sum 50 means 50 ones, tau=1.5. Bisection works. At tau low 1, sum100; high3 sum0. Good.

Now, exact evaluator may use `np.correlate` which for n=512 returns length 1023. Good.

Let's produce final code. Ensure final fence. No extra text. final.
</think>

```python
import time
import math
import numpy as np

_TERM_CACHE = {}
_GOLDPHI = (1.0 + math.sqrt(5.0)) / 2.0
_GOLDGR = _GOLDPHI - 1.0


def _term_cache(n):
    cache = _TERM_CACHE.get(n)
    if cache is None:
        N = 1
        need = 2 * int(n) - 1
        while N < need:
            N <<= 1
        cache = {
            'N': N,
            'd': np.arange(int(n), dtype=np.intp),
            'n_minus_d': np.arange(int(n), 0, -1, dtype=np.intp),
        }
        _TERM_CACHE[n] = cache
    return cache


def compute_terms(h):
    n = int(h.size)
    cache = _term_cache(n)
    H = np.fft.rfft(h, cache['N'])
    ac = np.fft.irfft(H * np.conj(H), cache['N'])[:n]

    pref = np.empty(n + 1, dtype=float)
    pref[0] = 0.0
    pref[1:] = np.cumsum(h, dtype=float)
    total = pref[-1]

    d = cache['d']
    idx = cache['n_minus_d']

    A = pref[idx] - ac
    B = total - pref[d] - ac
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    return A, B, dx


def fast_c5_from_terms(A, B, dx):
    m = float(A[0]) if A.size else 0.0
    if A.size > 1:
        ma = float(np.max(A[1:]))
        if ma > m:
            m = ma
        mb = float(np.max(B[1:]))
        if mb > m:
            m = mb
    if m < 0.0 and m > -1e-12:
        m = 0.0
    return m * float(dx)


def fast_c5(h):
    A, B, dx = compute_terms(h)
    return fast_c5_from_terms(A, B, dx)


def exact_c5(h):
    n = int(h.size)
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    val = float(np.max(corr)) * (2.0 / float(n))
    if val < 0.0 and val > -1e-12:
        val = 0.0
    return val


def repair_sum(h, S):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if h.size == 0:
        return h
    S = float(S)
    err = float(S - h.sum())
    if abs(err) <= 1e-12:
        return h

    idx = np.where((h > 1e-9) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-9))[0]
    if idx.size > 0:
        h[idx] += err / float(idx.size)
    else:
        if err > 0.0:
            order = np.argsort(h)
            rem = err
            for k in order:
                cap = 1.0 - h[k]
                if cap <= 0.0:
                    continue
                add = cap if cap < rem else rem
                h[k] += add
                rem -= add
                if rem <= 1e-14:
                    break
        else:
            order = np.argsort(-h)
            rem = -err
            for k in order:
                sub = h[k] if h[k] < rem else rem
                h[k] -= sub
                rem -= sub
                if rem <= 1e-14:
                    break

    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    err = float(S - h.sum())
    if abs(err) > 1e-13:
        idx = np.where((h > 1e-9) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-9))[0]
        if idx.size > 0:
            h[idx[0]] += err
        else:
            if err > 0.0:
                idx = np.where(h < 1.0)[0]
                if idx.size > 0:
                    h[idx[0]] = 1.0
            else:
                idx = np.where(h > 0.0)[0]
                if idx.size > 0:
                    h[idx[0]] = 0.0
    return h


def quick_feasible(z, S):
    h = np.array(z, dtype=float, copy=True)
    if h.size == 0:
        return h
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
        h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    h -= (float(h.sum()) - float(S)) / float(h.size)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return repair_sum(h, S)


def project_sum_box(z, S):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float)
    if z.size == 0:
        return z
    S = float(S)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)

    n = int(z.size)
    if S <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if S >= float(n):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=float)

    h = z - (float(z.sum()) - S) / float(n)
    if h.min() >= -1e-12 and h.max() <= 1.0 + 1e-12:
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
        return repair_sum(h, S)

    lo = float(np.min(z)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(z)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(70):
        tau = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        hh = np.clip(z - tau, 0.0, 1.0)
        s = float(hh.sum())
        if s > S:
            lo = tau
        else:
            hi = tau

    h = np.clip(z - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    return repair_sum(h, S)


def smooth_h(h, sigma):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if sigma <= 0.0 or h.size == 0:
        return h.copy()
    r = int(math.ceil(3.0 * float(sigma)))
    if r <= 0:
        return h.copy()
    x = np.arange(-r, r + 1, dtype=float)
    k = np.exp(-0.5 * (x / float(sigma)) ** 2)
    s = float(k.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return h.copy()
    k /= s
    hp = np.pad(h, r, mode='edge')
    out = np.convolve(hp, k, mode='same')[:h.size]
    return out


def resample_h(h0, n1, mode='linear'):
    h0 = np.asarray(h0, dtype=float)
    n0 = int(h0.size)
    n1 = int(n1)
    if n1 <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    if n0 == 1:
        return np.full(n1, float(h0[0]), dtype=float)
    if n0 == n1:
        return h0.copy()

    if mode == 'nearest':
        frac = (np.arange(n1) + 0.5) * (float(n0) / float(n1))
        idx = np.floor(frac).astype(np.intp)
        idx = np.clip(idx, 0, n0 - 1)
        h1 = h0[idx]
    else:
        x0 = (np.arange(n0) + 0.5) / float(n0)
        x1 = (np.arange(n1) + 0.5) / float(n1)
        h1 = np.interp(x1, x0, h0, left=h0[0], right=h0[-1])

    h1 = np.asarray(h1, dtype=float)
    return project_sum_box(h1, 0.5 * float(n1))


def make_best_start(parent, n, rng=None):
    h = resample_h(parent, n, 'linear')
    c = fast_c5(h)
    best = h

    if n > int(parent.size):
        h2 = resample_h(parent, n, 'nearest')
        c2 = fast_c5(h2)
        if c2 < c:
            best = h2
            c = c2

        sigma = max(0.5, 1.0 * math.sqrt(float(n) / float(parent.size)))
        h3 = smooth_h(best, sigma)
        h3 = project_sum_box(h3, 0.5 * float(n))
        c3 = fast_c5(h3)
        if c3 < c:
            best = h3
            c = c3

        if rng is not None and n > 16:
            h4 = quick_feasible(best + rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, n), 0.5 * float(n))
            c4 = fast_c5(h4)
            if c4 < c:
                best = h4
                c = c4
    elif rng is not None and n > 16:
        h4 = quick_feasible(best + rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, n), 0.5 * float(n))
        c4 = fast_c5(h4)
        if c4 < c:
            best = h4

    return best


def default_start(n, kind=0, rng=None):
    n = int(n)
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * 2.0 / float(n)
    if kind == 0:
        h = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(np.pi * x)
    elif kind == 1:
        h = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(np.pi * (x - 1.0))
    elif kind == 2:
        h = 0.5 + 0.4 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x) + 0.2 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    elif kind == 3:
        h = 0.5 + 0.4 * np.cos(np.pi * x) + 0.3 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    else:
        h = 0.5 * np.ones(n, dtype=float)
        if rng is not None:
            h = h + 0.2 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return project_sum_box(np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0), 0.5 * float(n))


def gradient_from_weights(h, wA, wB, dx):
    n = int(h.size)
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    nzA = np.nonzero(wA > 0.0)[0]
    for d in nzA:
        d = int(d)
        ww = float(wA[d])
        if ww == 0.0:
            continue
        if d == 0:
            g += ww * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
        else:
            mp = n - d
            g[:mp] += ww * (1.0 - h[d:])
            g[d:] -= ww * h[:-d]

    nzB = np.nonzero(wB > 0.0)[0]
    for d in nzB:
        d = int(d)
        ww = float(wB[d])
        if ww == 0.0:
            continue
        if d == 0:
            g += ww * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
        else:
            g[d:] += ww * (1.0 - h[:-d])
            g[:n - d] -= ww * h[d:]

    g *= float(dx)
    return g


def active_gradient(h, nu=0.02, K=None, rng=None, random_single_prob=0.15):
    A, B, dx = compute_terms(h)
    c = fast_c5_from_terms(A, B, dx)

    n = int(A.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    vals = np.empty(L, dtype=float)
    vals[:n] = A
    vals[n:] = B[1:]

    if K is None:
        K = max(4, min(128, max(4, L // 4)))
    K = int(min(K, L))
    if K < L:
        sel = np.argpartition(vals, -K)[-K:]
    else:
        sel = np.arange(L)

    sv = vals[sel]
    mx = float(sv.max())
    w = np.exp((sv - mx) / max(float(nu), 1e-8))
    wsum = float(w.sum())
    if wsum <= 0.0:
        w = np.ones(K, dtype=float)
    else:
        w = w / wsum

    if rng is not None:
        if random_single_prob > 0.0 and rng.random() < random_single_prob:
            w2 = np.zeros(K, dtype=float)
            w2[int(rng.choice(K, p=w))] = 1.0
        else:
            w2 = w.copy()
            if rng.random() < 0.35:
                mult = rng.uniform(0.5, 1.5, K)
                w2 = w2 * mult
                s = float(w2.sum())
                if s > 0.0:
                    w2 /= s
    else:
        w2 = w

    wA = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    wB = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for idx, ww in zip(sel, w2):
        idx = int(idx)
        if ww == 0.0:
            continue
        if idx < n:
            wA[idx] = ww
        else:
            wB[idx - n + 1] = ww

    g = gradient_from_weights(h, wA, wB, dx)
    return g, c, vals


def improve_pg(h, max_time=60.0, rng=None, nu=0.04, K=None, initial_step=0.10, deadline=None):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = int(h.size)
    if n < 2:
        c = fast_c5(h) if n else 0.0
        return h, c

    S = 0.5 * float(n)
    h = project_sum_box(h, S)
    c = fast_c5(h)

    if max_time <= 0.0:
        return h, c

    now = time.time()
    if deadline is None:
        local_deadline = now + float(max_time)
    else:
        local_deadline = min(now + float(max_time), float(deadline))

    step = float(initial_step)
    stagnant = 0
    iter_count = 0

    while time.time() < local_deadline:
        if local_deadline - time.time() < 10.0:
            break

        iter_count += 1
        g, c_new, _ = active_gradient(h, nu=nu, K=K, rng=rng, random_single_prob=0.25)
        c = c_new

        max_abs = float(np.max(np.abs(g))) if g.size else 0.0
        if max_abs < 1e-14:
            stagnant += 3
            continue

        direction = -g / max_abs
        t = step
        accepted = False
        accepted_h = None
        accepted_c = c

        for _ in range(12):
            if time.time() >= local_deadline:
                break
            cand = quick_feasible(h + t * direction, S)
            ctry = fast_c5(cand)
            if ctry < c - 1e-12:
                accepted = True
                accepted_h = cand
                accepted_c = ctry
                break
            t *= 0.5
            if t < 1e-5:
                break

        if accepted:
            h = accepted_h
            c = accepted_c
            stagnant = 0
            step = min(0.35, max(0.02, t * 1.5 + 0.01))
        else:
            stagnant += 1
            step = max(0.005, step * 0.6)

        if iter_count % 150 == 0:
            nu = max(0.004, nu * 0.8)

        if stagnant > 20:
            break

    h = project_sum_box(h, S)
    return h, c


def _pair_eval(h, i, j, a, b, t):
    x = a + t
    y = b - t
    if x < 0.0:
        x = 0.0
    elif x > 1.0:
        x = 1.0
    if y < 0.0:
        y = 0.0
    elif y > 1.0:
        y = 1.0

    oi = h[i]
    oj = h[j]
    h[i] = x
    h[j] = y
    c = fast_c5(h)
    h[i] = oi
    h[j] = oj
    return c


def pair_minimize(h, i, j, c_current, search_evals=8, tol=1e-12):
    if i == j:
        return h, c_current

    a = float(h[i])
    b = float(h[j])

    lo = max(-a, b - 1.0)
    hi = min(1.0 - a, b)
    if lo > 0.0:
        lo = 0.0
    if hi < 0.0:
        hi = 0.0

    span = hi - lo
    if span <= 1e-12:
        return h, c_current

    eps = 1e-3 * span
    if eps < 1e-6:
        eps = 1e-6
    if eps > 1e-2:
        eps = 1e-2

    best_t = 0.0
    best_f = float(c_current)
    pos_improved = False
    neg_improved = False

    tpos = hi
    if eps < hi:
        tpos = eps
    if tpos > lo + 1e-12:
        f = _pair_eval(h, i, j, a, b, tpos)
        if f < best_f - tol:
            best_f = f
            best_t = tpos
            pos_improved = True

    tneg = lo
    if -eps > lo:
        tneg = -eps
    if tneg < hi - 1e-12:
        f = _pair_eval(h, i, j, a, b, tneg)
        if f < best_f - tol:
            best_f = f
            best_t = tneg
            neg_improved = True

    if best_t == 0.0:
        return h, c_current

    lo_g = lo
    hi_g = hi
    if pos_improved and not neg_improved:
        lo_g = 0.0
    elif neg_improved and not pos_improved:
        hi_g = 0.0

    if hi_g <= lo_g + 1e-12:
        if best_f < float(c_current) - tol and best_t != 0.0:
            x = a + best_t
            y = b - best_t
            if x < 0.0:
                x = 0.0
            elif x > 1.0:
                x = 1.0
            if y < 0.0:
                y = 0.0
            elif y > 1.0:
                y = 1.0
            h[i] = x
            h[j] = y
            return h, best_f
        return h, c_current

    c1 = lo_g + _GOLDGR * (hi_g - lo_g)
    c2 = hi_g - _GOLDGR * (hi_g - lo_g)

    f1 = _pair_eval(h, i, j, a, b, c1)
    if f1 < best_f:
        best_f = f1
        best_t = c1

    f2 = _pair_eval(h, i, j, a, b, c2)
    if f2 < best_f:
        best_f = f2
        best_t = c2

    for _ in range(max(0, int(search_evals))):
        if f1 < f2:
            hi_g = c2
            c2 = c1
            f2 = f1
            c1 = lo_g + _GOLDGR * (hi_g - lo_g)
            f1 = _pair_eval(h, i, j, a, b, c1)
            if f1 < best_f:
                best_f = f1
                best_t = c1
        else:
            lo_g = c1
            c1 = c2
            f1 = f2
            c2 = hi_g - _GOLDGR * (hi_g - lo_g)
            f2 = _pair_eval(h, i, j, a, b, c2)
            if f2 < best_f:
                best_f = f2
                best_t = c2

        if hi_g - lo_g <= 1e-10 * max(1.0, abs(hi_g), abs(lo_g)):
            break

    for ttest in (0.5 * (lo_g + hi_g), lo_g, hi_g):
        if abs(float(ttest)) < 1e-14:
            continue
        if ttest < lo_g - 1e-12 or ttest > hi_g + 1e-12:
            continue
        f = _pair_eval(h, i, j, a, b, ttest)
        if f < best_f:
            best_f = f
            best_t = ttest

    if best_f < float(c_current) - tol and best_t != 0.0:
        x = a + best_t
        y = b - best_t
        if x < 0.0:
            x = 0.0
        elif x > 1.0:
            x = 1.0
        if y < 0.0:
            y = 0.0
        elif y > 1.0:
            y = 1.0
        h[i] = x
        h[j] = y
        return h, best_f

    return h, c_current


def improve_pairs(h, max_time=60.0, rng=None,
                  modes=('gradient', 'adjacent', 'opposite', 'random'),
                  search_evals=8, nu=0.02, K=None, deadline=None):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = int(h.size)
    if n < 2:
        c = fast_c5(h) if n else 0.0
        return h, c

    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)

    S = 0.5 * float(n)
    h = project_sum_box(h, S)
    c = fast_c5(h)

    if max_time <= 0.0:
        return h, c

    now = time.time()
    if deadline is None:
        local_deadline = now + float(max_time)
    else:
        local_deadline = min(now + float(max_time), float(deadline))

    pass_idx = 0
    iters = 0

    while time.time() < local_deadline:
        if local_deadline - time.time() < 10.0:
            break

        mode = modes[pass_idx % len(modes)]
        order = np.arange(n)

        if mode == 'random':
            rng.shuffle(order)
        elif mode == 'gradient' and rng.random() < 0.25:
            rng.shuffle(order)

        use_grad = False
        high_idx = None
        low_idx = None
        is_high = None
        is_low = None

        if mode == 'gradient':
            g, c_new, _ = active_gradient(h, nu=nu, K=K, rng=rng, random_single_prob=0.0)
            c = c_new
            gm = g - float(g.mean())
            if n > 4:
                q75 = float(np.quantile(gm, 0.75))
                q25 = float(np.quantile(gm, 0.25))
                high_idx = np.where((gm > q75) & (h > 1e-6))[0]
                low_idx = np.where((gm < q25) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-6))[0]
                if high_idx.size > 0 and low_idx.size > 0:
                    use_grad = True
                    is_high = np.zeros(n, dtype=bool)
                    is_low = np.zeros(n, dtype=bool)
                    is_high[high_idx] = True
                    is_low[low_idx] = True

        for i in order:
            if local_deadline - time.time() < 8.0:
                break

            ii = int(i)

            if mode == 'adjacent':
                jj = (ii + 1) % n
            elif mode == 'opposite':
                jj = (ii + n // 2) % n
                if jj == ii:
                    jj = (ii + 1) % n
            elif use_grad:
                if is_high[ii]:
                    if low_idx.size == 0:
                        jj = (ii + 1) % n
                    else:
                        jj = int(low_idx[int(rng.integers(low_idx.size))])
                        if jj == ii:
                            jj = (ii + 1) % n
                elif is_low[ii]:
                    if high_idx.size == 0:
                        jj = (ii + 1) % n
                    else:
                        jj = int(high_idx[int(rng.integers(high_idx.size))])
                        if jj == ii:
                            jj = (ii + 1) % n
                else:
                    jj = int(rng.integers(n))
                    if jj == ii:
                        jj = (ii + 1) % n
            else:
                jj = int(rng.integers(n))
                if jj == ii:
                    jj = (ii + 1) % n

            if jj == ii:
                continue

            h, c2 = pair_minimize(h, ii, jj, c, search_evals=int(search_evals))
            if c2 < c - 1e-12:
                c = c2

            iters += 1
            if (iters & 31) == 0 and local_deadline - time.time() < 12.0:
                break

        pass_idx += 1

    h = project_sum_box(h, S)
    return h, c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    budget = max(1.0, float(budget_s))

    if budget > 60.0:
        reserve = min(30.0, max(5.0, 0.02 * budget))
    else:
        reserve = max(0.5, 0.1 * budget)

    final_deadline = t0 + budget - reserve
    if final_deadline < t0 + 1.0:
        final_deadline = t0 + max(1.0, 0.9 * budget)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    best_h = None
    best_c = float('inf')
    best_n = 0

    pv = globals().get('initial_h_values', None)
    parent = None
    if pv is not None:
        try:
            p = np.asarray(pv, dtype=float).ravel()
        except Exception:
            p = None
        if p is not None and p.size >= 4 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1.0)
            if p.size % 2 != 0:
                parent = resample_h(p, p.size + 1, 'nearest')
            else:
                parent = project_sum_box(p, 0.5 * float(p.size))

    if parent is not None:
        cpar = exact_c5(parent)
        best_h = np.array(parent, dtype=float, copy=True)
        best_c = cpar
        best_n = int(parent.size)

    starts = []

    if parent is not None:
        base = int(parent.size)
        if base < 4:
            base = 64
        if base % 2 != 0:
            base += 1
        if base < 16:
            base = 16

        if budget >= 700.0:
            max_target = 512
        elif budget >= 350.0:
            max_target = 320
        elif budget >= 150.0:
            max_target = 160
        else:
            max_target = base
        max_target = max(max_target, base)

        cand_targets = [base]
        for t in (2 * base, 4 * base, 8 * base):
            if t <= max_target and t not in cand_targets:
                cand_targets.append(t)
        if 512 <= max_target and 512 not in cand_targets:
            cand_targets.append(512)
        if max_target not in cand_targets and max_target > 512:
            cand_targets.append(max_target)

        seen = set()
        for t in sorted(set(cand_targets)):
            t = int(t)
            if t < 4:
                continue
            if t % 2 != 0:
                t += 1
            if t in seen:
                continue
            seen.add(t)
            if t != int(parent.size):
                hstart = make_best_start(parent, t, rng)
            else:
                hstart = project_sum_box(parent, 0.5 * float(t))
            starts.append(hstart)
    else:
        if budget >= 700.0:
            tlist = [128, 256, 512]
        elif budget >= 350.0:
            tlist = [128, 256]
        elif budget >= 120.0:
            tlist = [128]
        else:
            tlist = [64]

        for t in tlist:
            best_start = None
            bc = float('inf')
            for kind in range(5):
                h0 = default_start(t, kind, rng)
                hc = fast_c5(h0)
                if hc < bc:
                    best_start = h0
                    bc = hc
            if best_start is not None:
                starts.append(best_start)

    if not starts:
        n = 64 if budget < 120.0 else 128
        starts.append(default_start(n, 0, rng))

    if time.time() < final_deadline - 10.0:
        total_opt = max(1.0, final_deadline - time.time())
        weights = [1.0, 1.7, 2.3, 3.0, 3.5, 4.0][:len(starts)]
        sw = float(sum(weights))

        for idx, h0 in enumerate(starts):
            if time.time() >= final_deadline - 15.0:
                break

            n0 = int(h0.size)
            alloc = (weights[idx] / sw) * total_opt
            if alloc < 10.0:
                htmp = project_sum_box(h0, 0.5 * float(n0))
                ce = exact_c5(htmp)
                if ce < best_c:
                    best_c = ce
                    best_h = np.array(htmp, dtype=float, copy=True)
                    best_n = n0
                continue

            pg_frac = 0.40 if n0 <= 160 else (0.30 if n0 <= 320 else 0.20)
            pg_time = alloc * pg_frac

            if pg_time > 20.0:
                hopt, copt = improve_pg(
                    h0,
                    max_time=pg_time,
                    rng=rng,
                    nu=0.04,
                    K=None,
                    deadline=min(final_deadline, time.time() + alloc - 5.0)
                )
            else:
                hopt, copt = h0, fast_c5(h0)

            if copt < best_c + 1e-5:
                ce = exact_c5(hopt)
                if ce < best_c:
                    best_c = ce
                    best_h = np.array(hopt, dtype=float, copy=True)
                    best_n = int(hopt.size)

            pair_time = max(1.0, alloc - pg_time - 5.0)
            if time.time() < final_deadline - 10.0:
                hopt, copt = improve_pairs(
                    hopt,
                    max_time=pair_time,
                    rng=rng,
                    deadline=min(final_deadline, time.time() + pair_time)
                )

            if copt < best_c + 1e-5:
                ce = exact_c5(hopt)
                if ce < best_c:
                    best_c = ce
                    best_h = np.array(hopt, dtype=float, copy=True)
                    best_n = int(hopt.size)

    if best_h is not None and time.time() < final_deadline - 40.0:
        extra = final_deadline - time.time() - 25.0
        if extra > 20.0:
            bn = int(best_h.size)
            if bn < 512:
                n2 = min(512, 2 * bn)
                if n2 < 4:
                    n2 = 64
                if n2 % 2 != 0:
                    n2 += 1
                hfinal = make_best_start(best_h, n2, rng)
            else:
                hfinal = np.array(best_h, dtype=float, copy=True)

            hfinal, cfinal = improve_pairs(
                hfinal,
                max_time=extra,
                rng=rng,
                deadline=final_deadline
            )

            if cfinal < best_c + 1e-5:
                ce = exact_c5(hfinal)
                if ce < best_c:
                    best_c = ce
                    best_h = np.array(hfinal, dtype=float, copy=True)
                    best_n = int(hfinal.size)

    if best_h is None:
        n = 128 if budget >= 120.0 else 64
        best_h = default_start(n, 0, rng)
        best_n = int(best_h.size)

    best_h = np.array(best_h, dtype=float, copy=True)
    best_h = project_sum_box(best_h, 0.5 * float(best_h.size))
    best_c = exact_c5(best_h)
    best_n = int(best_h.size)

    return best_h, float(best_c), best_n
```
